- **Main goal:** Athletic performance
- **Sport:** Soccer - midfielders (CM / DM / AM / wide) · Late off-season
- **Level:** Intermediate (L2): 2 years of consistent training
- **Plan type:** 8 weeks · Week 1-8
- **Start date:** 12 Oct 2026
- **Training days per week:** 3 · Monday, Wednesday, Friday · about 10-12 exercises a session (plus the warm-up and 2 core exercises)
- Terms and release of liability accepted before this plan was built: client, Spanish
- Health check: answered; nothing in it asked for a doctor first. [Official PAR-Q+ (in English)](https://eparmedx.com/)

## Before you start

- Your season is close. The last 2 weeks of this plan get you ready for it: more speed and power, fewer sets.
- Week 6 jumps more than usual (16 % more sets for Hips (glutes + hamstrings)): adjust if needed.
- Biceps, Triceps: one exercise of their own on the training days (the arm spot), with the same sets each week. They do not go up like the other groups. Add weight and reps instead.
- Your training days hold about 80% of your sport's weekly jumps (box, squat, long and quick jumps). The work still goes up every week. Add a station or a day if you want it all.
- Two-leg lifts are about 50 % of your leg sets. Your sport's guide is about 20 %. The coach's A and B days keep a two-leg squat and a two-leg hinge. Want more one-leg work? Use the one-leg swap for one of them.
- This plan does not count your 2 team practices a week. Think about them and change the weight yourself. Go lighter on a hard practice day. Do a bit more in a light week.
- Shoulders: 3 of its 11 final sets are on a bonus exercise (Seated DB Lateral Raise, Wednesday). Do it last that day. Your plan's lifts for it are already full. Skip it, and the shoulders total ends at about 8.

## Basic Instruction
> \*Always leave 1 rep in the tank, never work up to failure but get as close as you can.
>
> \*\*Do steady cardio whenever you want, just don't overdo it... I recommend doing it on your aerobic days, max 40 min of steady.
>
> \*\*\*The lower the reps, the higher the weight... Always go for 1-3 RIR (reps in reserve), aka RPE 7+.
>
> \*\*\*\*When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral and/or getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going to split grips (dumbbells, kettlebells, individual grips) helps take care of some stuff.
>
> \*\*\*\*\*If you have any questions just ask me - IG @BLAZECAMP or TWITTER @BLAZECAMP\_

_This plan starts with 3 reps in the tank. It ends with 1 in the last week. The coach's "1 in the tank" is where you finish. So follow the number for each week._

How hard each week is: weeks 1-3: leave 3 in the tank; week 4: leave 2 in the tank; week 5: leave 1-2 in the tank; week 6: leave 1 in the tank.

How heavy? Each exercise says how many reps to leave in the tank. "Leave 2 in the tank" means you could still do 2 more reps. That is about an 8 out of 10 for effort. (3 left is a 7. 1 left is a 9.) Your plan starts with 3 left. Then it goes to 2, then 1-2. It ends at 1 in the last week. Week 1: start a little lighter. Pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times.

Olympic lifts are for power. Cleans, snatches and their dumbbell and kettlebell versions train power. Move the weight as fast as you can, with good form. Pick a weight you can move fast on every rep. If it slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy. You do not need percentages for these. You only need them if you compete in weightlifting.

Core supersets are optional. You can pair a core exercise with any exercise to save time.

## How you progress

- Reps and weight follow a rule you apply yourself, not a number this plan promises. Every lift shows a range of reps. Example from your plan: Front Squat (barbell), 3 sets of 6-8. Say you hit 8 on every set, with good form and the planned reps left in the tank. Then add weight next time and start again at 6. Not there yet? Keep the weight and add a rep where you can.
- The plan adds sets week by week, like steps. Each muscle group reaches its final sets in the last week. The week-by-week table shows this. Some weeks keep the same sets as the week before. In those weeks, add reps or weight.
- Week 6 adds more than usual (over about 15 % more sets for a muscle group, or in total). It is marked ↑: adjust if needed.
- Your next block starts a little higher than this one did: about 5 % more sets, in whole sets. It never goes past the top number for your level. At the top, the sets stay. Then you add weight and reps.
- You can edit this plan. Change the sets of any exercise on any day or week. Add or take out exercises. Turn blocks off.

## How to adjust this plan

- Missed most of a week? Do that week again before you move on. Missed one day? Do it on the next free day, or let it go. Never squeeze two days into one.
- A set felt like your max, with no rep left? Next time, take one set off that exercise, or a little weight.
- Everything feels easy? First add a little weight or a rep. Never skip ahead more than one week.
- Something hurts? First use a listed swap, then cut that exercise's sets by a third to a half, then drop it for the rest of the block. See a professional if it keeps hurting.
- Life got in the way for a few weeks? Maybe you were sick, away or busy. Stop the block. Take the easy week if you want. Then build a new block.
- You or your coach can turn off these blocks on any day: jumps, throws and speed; power / Olympic lifts; core; cardio; accessories. You can also add or take out exercises, like more rear shoulder work. Use the suggested supersets to save time.
- Switching off the accessories block takes away every set just for Shoulders, Biceps, Triceps and Calves.
- You or your coach can also change the sets of any exercise, on any day or week. The plan is a starting point you can edit.
- The core block is optional. You may turn it off or change its exercises on any day. You can change anything in this plan.
- Each day shows an average time. It is an estimate, just so you know. Nothing in this plan is cut or changed because of time. Want a shorter day? Use the suggested supersets. Or take out what the day lists as easiest to take out.

## Optional add-ons

_Not part of your template. Add any of these if you want; the plan is complete without them._

- **Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue)** — 4-8 × 5-10 s · Extra work for your sport or activity.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Split-Step%20Reaction%20Drill%20(split-step%2C%20then%20shuffle%20%2F%20crossover%20%2F%20drop-step%20on%20a%20cue)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue)`
- **Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)** — 2-3 × 12-20 Reps · Extra work for your sport or activity.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20%2F%20Bent-Knee%20Calf%20Raise%20(soleus%3B%20machine%20or%20DBs%20on%20the%20knees)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)`
- **Single-Leg Balance Reach (front / side / back)** — 2-3 × 3 to 5 per side · Extra work for your sport or activity.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Balance%20Reach%20(front%20%2F%20side%20%2F%20back)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Leg Balance Reach (front / side / back)`
- **Tibialis Raise (back against a wall)** — 2-3 × 15-25 Reps · Extra work for your sport or activity.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Tibialis%20Raise%20(back%20against%20a%20wall)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Tibialis Raise (back against a wall)`
- **Farmer Carry (bilateral)** — 2-4 × 20-40 m (or 20-40 s) · Do it with your core or conditioning work. It does not count as muscle sets.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Farmer%20Carry%20(bilateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Farmer Carry (bilateral)`

Your plan climbs like a stairway. The steps are small up to week 3. They get a bit bigger from week 4. The hardest weeks come at the end.

## Week 1 — Easy start (small steps)

_Week level: 2 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**Baseline test this week (before you load up)** (Speed / agility, Jumps)

**Athletic work this week:** 54 jump contacts · 38 ankle-hop contacts · 20 throws · 54 direction changes · 180 m of sprints · 4 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 61% · Receive-Create 2 (jump stage 2): land and go

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 80-100 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) + Copenhagen Plank (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (saves about 5.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine), Dumbbell Curl_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Cable Woodchopper (cable chop)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Weighted Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Medball Rotational Throw - initiated from the hands (thorax-led) (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Cross-Body Mountain Climber (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Woodchopper%20(cable%20chop)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Cable Woodchopper (cable chop)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 10-30 s · hold with good form. Stop before your form slips · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Test**
1. **Test: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest. Write down the best.
   - First test this week. It shows where you start.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Two-Foot Lateral Step Hops (quick, side to side over a low step)** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the quick rebound)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Step%20Hops%20(quick%2C%20side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20low%20step)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Step Hops (quick, side to side over a low step)`
2. **Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat)** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Forward Plate Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Double Bounce - Bilateral (two quick pulses on landing, then go) (keeps the jump; loses the quick rebound)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Lateral%20Plate%20Hops%20(Forward%20On%20%2F%20Sideways%20Off-and-On%20%2F%20Forward%20Down%2C%20Repeat)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat)`
3. **Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)** — 2 × 10 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Ankle%20Hops%20(bilateral%2C%20in%20place)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast. If the bar slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power. No percentages unless your coach turns them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / High-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hang%20Power%20Clean%20-%20from%20the%20High%20Hang%20(hip%20crease)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 3-6 min rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Hex (Trap) Bar Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Pull-up** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, make it easier. Use the assisted machine. Or keep your feet on the floor, or do a ring or TRX row. No bands.
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Close-Grip Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Step-Up%20(forward%20and%20lateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Step-Up (forward and lateral)`

**Accessories**
1. **Dumbbell Curl** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dumbbell Curl`
2. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
3. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

### Tuesday · Aerobic day

- **Green (B): moderate · bike** · 20 min · 3 min hard / 3 min easy, repeat
  - Or use another of your cardio tools: run. Go by time, not distance.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 115-140 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (saves about 3 min)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Tate Press + Nordic Hamstring Curl (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (saves about 5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl, Tate Press · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **V-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=V-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `V-Up`

**Test**
1. **Test: 10-30 m sprint** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest. Write down the best.
   - First test this week. It shows where you start.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Land and go: land firm, then jump again right away. Reset between jumps
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Jump%20(side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20line%20or%20a%20low%20hurdle)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)`
2. **Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 9 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Forward%2FBackward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops`
3. **Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops** — 2 × 5 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Alternating%20Back-to-Wall%20Rotational%20Scoops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

**Accessories**
1. **Tate Press** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Nordic Hamstring Curl`

   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.

4. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`
5. **Seated DB Lateral Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Bonus exercise, done last. Your plan's lifts for this muscle group are already full. This one adds the last sets so you reach your final number.
   - Swap for: DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / DB Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Barbell Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses each hand moving on its own) / DB Lateral (Side) Raise (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated DB Lateral Raise`

_Superset to save time: Barbell Bench Press + Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift (saves about 5.5 min)_

**Warm-up**

2. **A-March (running drill)**

   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline DB Bench Press - Feet on Wall (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Barbell Bench Press`
2. **Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 3-6 min rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Barbell Deadlift (conventional) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hex%20(Trap)%20Bar%20Deadlift) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift`
3. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bird Dog Row (DB) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20DB%2FKB%20Row) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm DB/KB Row`
4. **Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-Elevated%20Lunge%20(RFE%20%2F%20Bulgarian%20split%20squat)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)`
5. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Walking Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`
6. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Close Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Lat%20Pulldown) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm Lat Pulldown`

**Accessories**

   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.

## Week 2 — Easy start (small steps)

_Week level: 3.3 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (Friday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift (Friday): 3 → 4 sets

**Athletic work this week:** 81 jump contacts · 46 ankle-hop contacts · 20 throws · 87 direction changes · 330 m of sprints · 4 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 54% · Receive-Create 2 (jump stage 2): land and go

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 80-100 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) + Copenhagen Plank (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (saves about 5.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone), Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine), Dumbbell Curl_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Weighted Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Medball Rotational Throw - initiated from the hands (thorax-led) (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Cross-Body Mountain Climber (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)

**Jumps, throws and speed**

It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. 4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest

## Week 3 — Building up

_Week level: 4.6 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Front Squat (barbell) (Monday): 3 → 4 sets
- +1 set on Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) (Monday): 3 → 4 sets

**Athletic work this week:** 87 jump contacts · 58 ankle-hop contacts · 24 throws · 96 direction changes · 390 m of sprints · 4 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 57% · Receive-Create 2 (jump stage 2): land and go

It trains the same thing another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Hops in Place (foot-speed / reactive hops) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. **Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast. If the bar slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power. No percentages unless your coach turns them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / High-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch)

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)

2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.

3. **Pull-up** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.

4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 115-140 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (saves about 3 min)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Tate Press + Nordic Hamstring Curl (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (saves about 5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops, Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 2 more_

**Strength**

   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses each hand moving on its own)

4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (effort 7 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.

It trains the same thing another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. 1. **Single-Arm DB Snatch** — 2 × 6/6 · move it fast. If the dumbbell slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest

   - Power work: move the dumbbell fast. Stop the set if it slows down.

   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline DB Bench Press - Feet on Wall (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle)

   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Barbell Deadlift (conventional) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load)

   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`

## Week 4 — Building up

_Week level: 6.7 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Step-Up (forward and lateral) (Monday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Single-Arm DB/KB Row (Friday): 3 → 4 sets
- +1 set on Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (Monday): 3 → 4 sets

**Athletic work this week:** 93 jump contacts · 68 ankle-hop contacts · 28 throws · 102 direction changes · 400 m of sprints · 5 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 60% · Receive-Create 2 (jump stage 2): land and go

_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (saves about 7 min)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone), Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine), Dumbbell Curl_

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push

_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (saves about 2.5 min)_

_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Tate Press + Nordic Hamstring Curl (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (saves about 5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops, Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 2 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

## Week 5 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 8.9 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Leg Extension (machine) (Monday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (Wednesday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Pull-up (Monday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Flat DB Bench Press (Wednesday): 3 → 4 sets
- +1 set on Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (Wednesday): 3 → 4 sets

**Athletic work this week:** 96 jump contacts · 72 ankle-hop contacts · 32 throws · 108 direction changes · 440 m of sprints · 6 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 60% · Receive-Create 2 (jump stage 2): land and go

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 105-130 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) + Copenhagen Plank (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (saves about 7 min)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (saves about 5.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone), Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine), Dumbbell Curl_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Cable Woodchopper (cable chop)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Weighted Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Medball Rotational Throw - initiated from the hands (thorax-led) (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Cross-Body Mountain Climber (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Woodchopper%20(cable%20chop)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Cable Woodchopper (cable chop)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 10-30 s · hold with good form. Stop before your form slips · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Two-Foot Lateral Step Hops (quick, side to side over a low step)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the quick rebound)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Step%20Hops%20(quick%2C%20side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20low%20step)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Step Hops (quick, side to side over a low step)`
2. **Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Forward Plate Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Double Bounce - Bilateral (two quick pulses on landing, then go) (keeps the jump; loses the quick rebound)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Lateral%20Plate%20Hops%20(Forward%20On%20%2F%20Sideways%20Off-and-On%20%2F%20Forward%20Down%2C%20Repeat)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat)`
3. **Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)** — 2 × 18 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Ankle%20Hops%20(bilateral%2C%20in%20place)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)`
4. **L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)** — 3 × 4 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - 3 cuts a rep
   - Swap for: Lateral Shuffles (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Hops in Place (foot-speed / reactive hops) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=L-Test%20%2F%20L-Drill%20(3-Cone)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast. If the bar slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power. No percentages unless your coach turns them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / High-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hang%20Power%20Clean%20-%20from%20the%20High%20Hang%20(hip%20crease)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 4 × 8-10 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Hex (Trap) Bar Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Pull-up** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, make it easier. Use the assisted machine. Or keep your feet on the floor, or do a ring or TRX row. No bands.
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Close-Grip Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Step-Up%20(forward%20and%20lateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Step-Up (forward and lateral)`

**Accessories**
1. **Dumbbell Curl** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dumbbell Curl`
2. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
3. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

### Tuesday · Aerobic day
_Optional: you can turn off this cardio, or move it to another day._
- **Green (B): moderate · bike** · 28 min · 3 min hard / 3 min easy, repeat
  - Or use another of your cardio tools: run. Go by time, not distance.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 130-160 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (saves about 3 min)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (saves about 5.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Tate Press + Nordic Hamstring Curl (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (saves about 5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops, Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 2 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **V-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=V-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `V-Up`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 2 × 5 x 20 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Falling-Start Accelerations`
2. **Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Land and go: land firm, then jump again right away. Reset between jumps
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Jump%20(side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20line%20or%20a%20low%20hurdle)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)`
3. **Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 18 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Forward%2FBackward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops`
4. **Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops** — 2 × 8 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Alternating%20Back-to-Wall%20Rotational%20Scoops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

**Accessories**
1. **Tate Press** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension (keeps the triceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Floor Dumbbell Skullcrusher (keeps the triceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Overhead Cable Triceps Extension (keeps the triceps work; loses the free-weight balance work) / Cable Triceps Pushdown - straight bar, supinated (reverse) grip (keeps the triceps work; loses the free-weight balance work)
