2. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl (keeps the hamstring curl; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Seated Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lying Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Nordic Hamstring Curl`

   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Delt DB Row (keeps the rear-delt work; loses the same direction of movement) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly`
4. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the top-end load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`
5. **Seated DB Lateral Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (effort 8-9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Bonus exercise, done last. Your plan's lifts for this muscle group are already full. This one adds the last sets so you reach your final number.

_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Leg Raise (lying / supine) (saves about 2.5 min)_

   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.

   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set

## Week 6 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 11 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**Last test this week: each test takes the place of that day's sprint, cutting or jump station** (Speed / agility, Jumps)

_This week jumps more than usual (16 % more sets for Hips (glutes + hamstrings)): adjust if needed._

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Step-Up (forward and lateral) (Monday): 3 → 4 sets
- +1 set on Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (Wednesday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Nordic Hamstring Curl (Wednesday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Single-Arm Lat Pulldown (Friday): 2 → 3 sets
- +1 set on Machine Calf Raise (Wednesday): 3 → 4 sets

**Athletic work this week:** 54 jump contacts · 38 ankle-hop contacts · 20 throws · 51 direction changes · 120 m of sprints · 4 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 61% · Receive-Create 2 (jump stage 2): land and go Test week: jumps, throws and sprints are lighter, so you test fresh.

   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dumbbell Curl`
2. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
3. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

### Tuesday · Aerobic day
_Optional: you can turn off this cardio, or move it to another day._
- **Green (B): moderate · bike** · 30 min · 3 min hard / 3 min easy, repeat
  - Or use another of your cardio tools: run. Go by time, not distance.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 135-165 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (saves about 3 min)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (saves about 5.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (saves about 5.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Tate Press + Nordic Hamstring Curl (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (saves about 6 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl, Tate Press · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **V-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=V-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `V-Up`

**Test**
1. **Test: 10-30 m sprint** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest. Write down the best.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Land and go: land firm, then jump again right away. Reset between jumps
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Jump%20(side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20line%20or%20a%20low%20hurdle)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)`
2. **Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 9 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Forward%2FBackward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops`
3. **Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops** — 2 × 5 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Alternating%20Back-to-Wall%20Rotational%20Scoops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.

4. **Machine Calf Raise** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the top-end load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`
5. **Seated DB Lateral Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Bonus exercise, done last. Your plan's lifts for this muscle group are already full. This one adds the last sets so you reach your final number.
   - Swap for: DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / DB Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Barbell Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses each hand moving on its own) / DB Lateral (Side) Raise (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated DB Lateral Raise`

### Friday · S3 - Olympic + strength
_Average time: about 120-145 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Leg Raise (lying / supine) (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Barbell Bench Press + Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift (saves about 7 min)_
_Superset to save time: Single-Arm DB/KB Row + Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (saves about 5.5 min)_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Star Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Lateral Crunch (keeps the core work; loses the held position)

It trains the same thing another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

It trains the same thing another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Four-Square Formation (4-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

It trains the same thing another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. **Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Single-Arm DB Snatch** — 2 × 4/4 · move it fast. If the dumbbell slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Power work: move the dumbbell fast. Stop the set if it slows down.

   - Swap for: Mid-Hang Snatch Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / High-Hang Snatch Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Staggered-Stance DB Hang Snatch (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline DB Bench Press - Feet on Wall (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle)

   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hex%20(Trap)%20Bar%20Deadlift) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift`

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bird Dog Row (DB) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20DB%2FKB%20Row) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm DB/KB Row`

   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-Elevated%20Lunge%20(RFE%20%2F%20Bulgarian%20split%20squat)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)`
5. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (effort 9 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`

   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set

## Week 7 — Pre-season: more speed and power

**Athletic work this week:** 96 jump contacts · 64 ankle-hop contacts · 24 throws · 108 direction changes · 440 m of sprints · 6 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 61% · Receive-Create 3 (jump stage 3): jumps in a row

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 85-100 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Bicycle Crunch + Copenhagen Plank (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Curl + Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone), Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top, Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time, Dumbbell Curl_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Skip (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Bicycle Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Rotational Crunch Chop Midline (horizontal) - Supine, Object Squeezed Between Knees (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Tall-Kneeling Cable Crunch Chop Midline (horizontal) - Object Squeezed Between Knees (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Star V-Ups (cross-body / diagonal V-ups) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Weighted Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)

2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 10-30 s · hold with good form. Stop before your form slips · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - Swap for: Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Single-Leg Forward Zig-Zag Jumps (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Single-Leg Lateral Plate Hops - Inner/Outer Leg (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load)

2. **Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - Swap for: Bilateral Forward Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Single-Leg Plate Hops (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Bilateral Forward Plate Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Right-Angle%20Hurdle%20Hops%20(Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral)`
3. **Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)** — 2 × 16 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

4. **L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)** — 3 × 4 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - 3 cuts a rep
   - Swap for: Lateral Shuffles (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Hops in Place (foot-speed / reactive hops) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Low-Hang Clean Pull** — 3 × 4 Reps · move it fast. If the bar slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power. No percentages unless your coach turns them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control

It trains the same thing another way) / High-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Low-Hang%20Clean%20Pull) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Low-Hang Clean Pull`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Before the season: fewer sets, with faster and more powerful moves
   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Hex (Trap) Bar Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Pull-up** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, make it easier. Use the assisted machine. Or keep your feet on the floor, or do a ring or TRX row. No bands.
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Close-Grip Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Step-Up%20(forward%20and%20lateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Step-Up (forward and lateral)`

   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly`
4. **Machine Calf Raise** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the top-end load)

### Friday · S3 - Olympic + strength

## Week 8 — Pre-season: more speed and power

**Athletic work this week:** 96 jump contacts · 64 ankle-hop contacts · 24 throws · 108 direction changes · 440 m of sprints · 6 Olympic-lift sets · Quick jumps: 61% · Receive-Create 3 (jump stage 3): jumps in a row

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull

1. **Dumbbell Curl** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dumbbell Curl`
2. **Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
3. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

### Tuesday · Aerobic day
_Optional: you can turn off this cardio, or move it to another day._
- **Green (B): moderate · bike** · 20-30 min · 3 min hard / 3 min easy, repeat
  - Or use another of your cardio tools: run. Go by time, not distance.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 80-100 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + Knees to Chest (hanging) (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Rotational Downward Slam (saves about 3 min)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press - palms facing each other (neutral grip) + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Tate Press + Nordic Hamstring Curl (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (saves about 3.5 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Rotational Downward Slam, Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops, Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Skip (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-Skip%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-Skip (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **Knees to Chest (hanging)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and slow. Stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: V-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Knees%20to%20Chest%20(hanging)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Knees to Chest (hanging)`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 2 × 5 x 20 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-March (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Falling-Start Accelerations`
2. **Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Repeat: do 2-3 big jumps in a row, then reset your arms. For 7 jumps, do 3 + 2 + 2. Jump as hard as you can each time
   - Swap for: Single-Leg Box Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Single-Leg Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Unilateral Hop On To (single-leg hop onto a plate/low box) (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Lateral Box Step-up Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Jump%20(side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20line%20or%20a%20low%20hurdle)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)`
3. **Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 16 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · rest 5 to 10 times as long as you work
   - Quick jumps in a row: stay bouncy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Backward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops`
4. **Medball Rotational Downward Slam** — 2 × 6 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Medball Squat to Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Rotational%20Downward%20Slam) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Rotational Downward Slam`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press - palms facing each other (neutral grip)** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Before the season: fewer sets, with faster and more powerful moves
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same main muscle; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Staggered-Stance%20(Kickstand)%20DB%20Deadlift) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift`

**Accessories**
1. **Tate Press** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension (keeps the triceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Floor Dumbbell Skullcrusher (keeps the triceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Overhead Cable Triceps Extension (keeps the triceps work; loses the free-weight balance work) / Cable Triceps Pushdown - straight bar, supinated (reverse) grip (keeps the triceps work; loses the free-weight balance work)

2. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl (keeps the hamstring curl; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Seated Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lying Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the feel of the original implement)

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly`
4. **Machine Calf Raise** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the top-end load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`

### Friday · S3 - Olympic + strength
_Average time: about 100-120 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or take out exercises._
_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Reverse Crunch (saves about 2.5 min)_
_Superset to save time: Barbell Bench Press + Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift (saves about 4 min)_
_Superset to save time: Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top + Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (saves about 4 min)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle, Lateral Shuffles, Single-Leg Broad Jump · small-muscle accessories: Barbell Calf Raise_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Skip (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-Skip%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-Skip (running drill)`

   - Swap for: Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Star Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Lateral Crunch (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20Plank%20(elbow)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side Plank (elbow)`

   - Swap for: Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / V-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

   - Swap for: Falling-Start Accelerations (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Acceleration Starts (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / A-March (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

   - Repeat: do 2-3 big jumps in a row, then reset your arms. For 7 jumps, do 3 + 2 + 2. Jump as hard as you can each time
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Single-Leg Box Jump (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Unilateral Hop On To (single-leg hop onto a plate/low box) (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Lateral Box Step-up Jump (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)

It trains the same thing another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Four-Square Formation (4-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

   - 2 cuts a rep
   - Swap for: Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill. It trains the same thing another way)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Single-Arm DB Snatch** — 3 × 4/4 · move it fast. If the dumbbell slows down or your form slips, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest

   - Power work: move the dumbbell fast. Stop the set if it slows down.

   - Swap for: Barbell Deadlift (conventional) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load)

3. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bird Dog Row (DB) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)

   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Walking Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

5. **Side (Lateral) Lunge - 1-second pause at the bottom** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - When your body weight feels easy at this effort, hold dumbbells at your sides (or a bar on your back)
   - Swap for: Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Walking Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`
6. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Close Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Lat%20Pulldown) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm Lat Pulldown`

**Accessories**
1. **Barbell Calf Raise** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (effort 8 out of 10) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Barbell Calf Raise`

### Saturday · Optional day
_Average time: about 40-45 min (an estimate, just so you know)_
_You can turn off these blocks today: core, cardio. You can also add or take out exercises._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form. Stop before your form slips · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: High Plank (straight-arm plank) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Medball Plank Rockers (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / L-Sit Hold (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Plank%20(forearm%20%2F%20low%20plank)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Plank (forearm / low plank)`

**Aerobic**
1. **Orange (C): long / easy · run** — 30-40 min · steady pace: you can still talk
   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.
   - This cardio is part of the plan, even if you skip the optional lifting.
